## First bounded implementation slice: B-IO repository mutation grouping

Approved protocol remains reports/io-group-commit-cache-design.md. This slice is NOT the full queue/coordinator/service integration or an admission authorization. Preserve finite family backlog in execution-preflight.md; do not rediscover it indefinitely or certify it from repository tests.

Direct source inspection:
- service.js healthy beginAttempt performs fresh gate/price/coverage + insert synchronously, without yield. Every completeAttempt reads the original stored price and finalizes synchronously. Analytics projection follows ACK. Unhealthy/unlimited exception requires independent persisted proof and durable sidecar gap; not groupable as ordinary healthy DB authority.
- requestAccounting.js beforeAttempt relies on no-yield fresh caller/config gate; any future queue wait must deliberately revalidate at dequeue. A raw repository group must never become a replacement gate.
- apiKeyAccountingRepo.js wraps insert/finalize individually in durableTransaction. Calling them within a group currently throws outermost-only; extract private mutation bodies while retaining existing public synchronous wrapper behavior.
- finalize conflict observation is intentionally committed before throwing; group result handling must retain that audit rather than rollback it as an ordinary failed command. Validation/known conflict should not poison unrelated commands, but group commit uncertainty must reject every member ACK.
- nativeTransactions uses savepoint/release, WAL/FULL verification, poison on release uncertainty; SQL.js durableTransaction exports/persists after transaction and forbids nesting. Ordinary per-command savepoints inside one durable group may isolate known refusal, but no per-row durableTransaction/fsync is permitted.
- driver existing owned adapter lifecycle and global identity govern writer exclusion/reopen. Future coordinator keyed to owned adapter; no new multiwriter or request state on executor singleton.

Integration requirement: no generic queued callback authority. Use internal typed prepared service operations with module-private authority resolution at dequeue and mutation in the same synchronous group transaction; never prepare commands at enqueue and trust them after await, never call nested per-row durable methods within group. Healthy admission must recheck original pricing equality/identity/policy/coverage/starttime; changed authority refuses rather than silently switching. Sidecar/unhealthy-unlimited path retains independent sole ACK and cannot be reinterpreted as ordinary group success. Queue-only helper cannot implement these checks; repo/service/typed preparation seam still requires bounded tests. Exact settlement derives from stored original snapshot at dequeue and projection follows ACK. No implementation or benchmark gain claim from this test file. Separate unused queue foundation controls extended10cases (byte ceiling/split, malformed result and async apply no ACK), plus6payload cases; `b394bebde` PASS16/162files/no skips, TEST/LINT/WHITESPACE_EXIT0, `/tmp/9router-accounting-coordinator-bounds-x1AxCJ/results.json`; no production changes. Active LSP4paths (coordinator, mock lifecycle, real durability, queued-service tests) confirmed clean. This is mock queue scheduling/payload evidence, not identity/config/policy/pricing admission or performance/actual-adapter certification. Queue byte bounds do not prove memory in bytes of all JS overhead or hostile same-process sandboxing. Queue thresholds/coalescing tunables and performance acceptance must be measured/disclosed before gain claims. Group foundation without consumers does not close B-IO; family integrations/B1–B7 and later adapter/crash/performance gates remain open.
